{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"nones","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"Nones","tags":["alternative"]},{"form":"Non.","tags":["alternative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The third day before the nones of March is March 5th; the third nones of August is August 3rd; and the third of the nones of November is November 3rd.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The notional first-quarter day of a Roman month, occurring on the 7th day of the four original 31-day months (March, May, Quintilis or July, and October) and on the 5th day of all other months."],"tags":["capitalized","historical","often"]},{"glosses":["The ninth hour after dawn (about 3 pm)."],"synonyms":[{"word":"none"},{"tags":["obsolete"],"word":"noon"}],"tags":["capitalized","historical","sometimes"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The Greek monks always listen to their reader recite Psalms 83, 84, and 85 from the Septuagint at nones.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The divine office appointed to the hour."],"synonyms":[{"word":"none"}],"topics":["Christianity"]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"noon"}],"glosses":["Alternative form of noon: the sixth hour after dawn; midday (12 pm)."],"tags":["alt-of","alternative","obsolete"]},{"glosses":["Synonym of lunch: a meal eaten around noon."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["synonym","synonym-of"],"word":"lunch"}],"tags":["obsolete"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/nəʊnz/","tags":["UK"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"sixth hour of daylight","word":"midday"},{"word":"noon"},{"word":"noontide"}],"upstream_line":232007,"upstream_offset":1138062561,"upstream_record_sha256":"c87e44255318e7ae5a468fc2610da8e222479e9295f99adf00fcf116179344f2","word":"nones"}
